   347	
   348	def _rule_pack_id(sequence: int) -> uuid.UUID:
   349	    return uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_URL, f"{_RULE_PACK_ID_URL_PREFIX}{sequence}")
   350	
   351	
   352	def _fail(message: str) -> None:
   353	    raise SystemExit(f"fold_pack_seq21: {message}")
   354	
   355	
   356	def _canon(obj: Any) -> str:
   357	    return json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
   358	
   359	
   360	def _nodes(node: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   361	    """Every dict in a condition tree, in a deterministic pre-order."""
   362	    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   363	    if isinstance(node, dict):
   364	        out.append(node)
   365	        for value in node.values():
   366	            out.extend(_nodes(value))
   367	    elif isinstance(node, list):
   368	        for value in node:
   369	            out.extend(_nodes(value))
   370	    return out
   371	
   372	
   373	def _rules_by_id(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   374	    return {rule["rule_id"]: rule for rule in payload["rules"]}
   375	
   376	
   377	def _products_by_code(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   378	    return {product["product_code"]: product for product in payload["products"]}
   379	
   380	
   381	def _required_facts(when: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
   382	    """The sorted, de-duplicated fact paths a condition tree reads.
   383	
   384	    Derived from the built condition rather than declared beside it, because
   385	    the compiler's ``_check_required_facts_match_condition`` compares exactly
   386	    these two and a hand-written list is one edit away from disagreeing.
   387	    """
   388	    facts = {node["fact"] for node in _nodes(when) if isinstance(node.get("fact"), str)}
   389	    return sorted(facts)
   390	
   391	
   392	def assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact(
   393	    signed_envelope: dict[str, Any],
   394	    digest: str,
   395	    *,
   396	    observed_at: datetime | None = None,
   397	) -> None:
   398	    """Require the chain anchor to be a SIGNED seq-20 pack, not just a digest.
   399	
   400	    Same shape as ``fold_pack_seq20.assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact``
   401	    — that one is pinned to sequence 19 by its own final check, so it is
   402	    reproduced here pinned to 20 rather than parameterised after the fact.
   403	    """
   404	    try:
   405	        trust_store = StaticTrustStore.from_env()
   406	    except RulePackVerificationError as exc:
   407	        _fail(
   408	            f"cannot verify the seq-20 anchor's signature: {exc}. Export the "
   409	            "production trust store (the public key, e.g. "
   410	            'VISA_ENGINE_TRUST_STORE_KEYS_JSON=\'[{"kid": "prod-2026-07-1", ...}]\') '
   411	            "and re-run — the anchor is never taken on a digest constant alone."
   412	        )
   413	    try:
   414	        verified = verify_rule_pack(
   415	            signed_envelope,
   416	            trust_store=trust_store,
   417	            observed_at=observed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
   418	        )
   419	    except RulePackVerificationError as exc:
   420	        _fail(f"the seq-20 signed bundle does not verify: {exc}")
   421	
   422	    signed_digest = verified.payload_sha256.hex()
   423	    if signed_digest != digest:
   424	        _fail(
   425	            f"the seq-20 SOURCE digest {digest} is not the digest of the signed "
   426	            f"seq-20 artifact ({signed_digest}) — the source on disk and the "
   427	            "artifact production verifies are two different payloads."
   428	        )
   429	    if verified.pack.payload.sequence != 20:
   430	        _fail(
   431	            "the signed bundle handed in as the seq-20 anchor carries sequence "
   432	            f"{verified.pack.payload.sequence}"
   433	        )
   434	
   435	
   436	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   437	# Pre-flight census checks — each edit's premise, verified against the base
   438	# pack rather than trusted from the docstring.
   439	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   440	
   441	
   442	def _eligibility_rule_ids_for_product(payload: dict[str, Any], product_code: str) -> set[str]:
   443	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   444	    if product_code not in products:
   445	        _fail(f"product {product_code!r} is not in the catalogue")
   446	    version_id = products[product_code]["product_version_id"]
   447	    return {
   448	        rule["rule_id"]
   449	        for rule in payload["rules"]
   450	        if rule["stage"] == "ELIGIBILITY" and version_id in rule.get("product_version_ids", [])
   451	    }
   452	
   453	
   454	def _assert_target_products_have_no_eligibility_rule(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   455	    """The nine products this fold unblocks must currently carry ZERO
   456	    ELIGIBILITY rules.
   457	
   458	    That is the whole claim — "structurally unrecommendable, because
   459	    ``declared_coverage`` is empty". If a base pack has meanwhile given one of
   460	    them a SUPPORT rule, this fold would be adding a SECOND, differently-keyed
   461	    route to the same product without anyone comparing the two; refuse instead.
   462	    """
   463	    for code in TARGET_PRODUCT_CODES:
   464	        existing = _eligibility_rule_ids_for_product(payload, code)
   465	        if existing:
   466	            _fail(
   467	                f"product {code!r} already carries ELIGIBILITY rule(s) "
   468	                f"{sorted(existing)} in the base pack — this fold's premise is "
   469	                "that the nine target products have none, so it refuses rather "
   470	                "than adding a second, unreconciled route to the same product"
   471	            )
   472	
   473	
   474	def _assert_retired_review_rules_are_dormant(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   475	    """Every rule this fold retires must be a REQUIRE_REVIEW rule gated on
   476	    ``intent.requested_product_code``, scoped to the product it is declared
   477	    against.
   478	
   479	    The retirement's justification is that the rule is dormant BY
   480	    CONSTRUCTION (``fact-mapper.ts`` never sets that fact) and that its review
   481	    question is now a fact the new SUPPORT rule tests. If a future base pack
   482	    gives one of them a condition that can actually fire from the browser,
   483	    retiring it would drop a live gate — so this refuses rather than deleting.
   484	    """
   485	    rules_by_id = _rules_by_id(payload)
   486	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   487	    for rule_id, product_code in RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES.items():
   488	        rule = rules_by_id.get(rule_id)
   489	        if rule is None:
   490	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r} is not in the base pack — the edit set is stale")
   491	        if rule["effect"]["type"] != "REQUIRE_REVIEW":
   492	            _fail(
   493	                f"{rule_id!r} is a {rule['effect']['type']} rule, not REQUIRE_REVIEW — "
   494	                "refusing to retire it under an edit that only claims to retire "
   495	                "dormant review gates"
   496	            )
   497	        if REQUESTED_PRODUCT_FACT not in (rule.get("required_facts") or []):
   498	            _fail(
   499	                f"{rule_id!r} no longer reads {REQUESTED_PRODUCT_FACT} — it can now "
   500	                "fire from the browser, so retiring it would drop a live gate"
   501	            )
   502	        version_id = products[product_code]["product_version_id"]
   503	        if version_id not in rule.get("product_version_ids", []):
   504	            _fail(
   505	                f"{rule_id!r} is not scoped to {product_code} — the declared "
   506	                "rule/product pairing is stale"
   507	            )
   508	
   509	
   510	def _bound_nodes(rule: dict[str, Any], fact: str, op: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   511	    return [
   512	        node
   513	        for node in _nodes(rule.get("when"))
   514	        if node.get("fact") == fact and node.get("op") == op
   515	    ]
   516	
   517	
   518	def _e33_guarantee_bounds(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:
   519	    """``(deposit_usd_minimum, property_usd_minimum)``, READ from the two E33
   520	    SUPPORT rules that already encode them.
   521	
   522	    The named-cause EXCLUDE this fold adds says "below the threshold". If the
   523	    number it uses were typed here it could drift from the number the SUPPORT
   524	    rule uses, and the pack would simultaneously deny a product for being
   525	    below X and grant it at Y. Extracting both makes that impossible and turns
   526	    a moved bound into a fold abort.
   527	    """
   528	    rules_by_id = _rules_by_id(payload)
   529	    bounds: list[int] = []
   530	    for rule_id, fact in (
   531	        (E33_DEPOSIT_DONOR_RULE_ID, DEPOSIT_FACT),
   532	        (E33_PROPERTY_DONOR_RULE_ID, PROPERTY_FACT),
   533	    ):
   534	        rule = rules_by_id.get(rule_id)
   535	        if rule is None:
   536	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r} is missing — cannot derive the E33 guarantee bound")
   537	        nodes = _bound_nodes(rule, fact, "gte")
   538	        if len(nodes) != 1:
   539	            _fail(
   540	                f"rule {rule_id!r} carries {len(nodes)} '{fact} gte' bounds; exactly "
   541	                "one is required to mirror it unambiguously"
   542	            )
   543	        value = nodes[0]["value"]
   544	        if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value <= 0:
   545	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r}'s '{fact} gte' bound is {value!r}, not a positive integer")
   546	        bounds.append(value)
   547	    return bounds[0], bounds[1]
   548	
   549	
   550	def _assert_employment_sponsor_scope_is_coherent(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   551	    """Every product the employment EXCLUDE is scoped to must declare
   552	    EMPLOYMENT among its ``covered_purposes``.
   553	
   554	    A hard filter conditioned on ``intent.purposes intersects [EMPLOYMENT]``
   555	    that is scoped to a product which cannot cover EMPLOYMENT in the first
   556	    place is dead weight at best and a misleading exclusion reason at worst.
   557	    """
   558	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   559	    for code in EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_PRODUCT_CODES:
   560	        if code not in products:
   561	            _fail(
   562	                f"product {code!r} is not in the catalogue — cannot scope {EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_RULE_ID}"
   563	            )
   564	        covered = products[code].get("covered_purposes") or []
   565	        if "EMPLOYMENT" not in covered:
   566	            _fail(
   567	                f"product {code!r} does not cover EMPLOYMENT ({covered}) — "
   568	                f"{EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_RULE_ID} would be scoped to a product its own "
   569	                "condition can never concern"
   570	            )
   571	    if EMPLOYER_IS_INDONESIAN_FACT not in {
   572	        node.get("fact") for rule in payload["rules"] for node in _nodes(rule.get("when"))
   573	    }:
   574	        _fail(
   575	            f"no rule in the base pack reads {EMPLOYER_IS_INDONESIAN_FACT} — the "
   576	            "fact this exclusion turns on is not one the interview establishes, "
   577	            "so the new rule could never fire"
   578	        )
   579	
   580	
   581	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   582	# The edits themselves.
   583	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   584	
   585	
   586	def _sponsor_premise_values(row: dict[str, Any]) -> set[str] | None:
   587	    for node in row["premises"]:
   588	        if node.get("fact") != "sponsor.type":
   589	            continue
   590	        if node["op"] == "eq":
   591	            return {node["value"]}
   592	        if node["op"] == "in":
   593	            return set(node["values"])
   594	        _fail(f"{row['rule_id']}: unsupported sponsor.type premise op {node['op']!r}")
   595	    return None
   596	
   597	
   598	def _assert_sponsor_premises_follow_the_catalogue(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   599	    """A SUPPORT rule may only admit sponsor categories its product's catalogue
   600	    record names. A wider premise would RECOMMEND a product on a sponsor answer
   601	    the product itself says it does not take."""
   602	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   603	    for row in SUPPORT_RULES:
   604	        admitted = _sponsor_premise_values(row)
   605	        if admitted is None:
   606	            continue
   607	        catalogue = set(products[row["product_code"]]["sponsor_types"])
   608	        if not admitted <= catalogue:
   609	            _fail(
   610	                f"{row['rule_id']} admits sponsor.type {sorted(admitted - catalogue)} that "
   611	                f"{row['product_code']}'s catalogue sponsor_types {sorted(catalogue)} does not name"
   612	            )
   613	
   614	
   615	def build_support_rule(payload: dict[str, Any], row: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   616	    """One ELIGIBILITY/SUPPORT rule, with its scope, covered purposes and
   617	    citations DERIVED from the product catalogue rather than transcribed."""
   618	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   619	    code = row["product_code"]
   620	    if code not in products:
   621	        _fail(f"product {code!r} is not in the catalogue — cannot build {row['rule_id']!r}")
   622	    product = products[code]
   623	    purposes = list(row["purposes"])
   624	    unknown = [
   625	        purpose for purpose in purposes if purpose not in (product.get("covered_purposes") or [])
   626	    ]
   627	    if unknown:
   628	        _fail(
   629	            f"{row['rule_id']!r} would cover {unknown} for {code}, which the product "
   630	            f"catalogue does not list among its covered_purposes "
   631	            f"({product.get('covered_purposes')})"
   632	        )
   633	    when = {
   634	        "op": "all",
   635	        "args": [
   636	            {"op": "intersects", "fact": "intent.purposes", "values": purposes},
   637	            *copy.deepcopy(row["premises"]),
   638	            {"op": "eq", "fact": row["qualifying_fact"], "value": True},
   639	        ],
   640	    }
   641	    return {
   642	        "when": when,
   643	        "scope": "PRODUCTS",
   644	        "stage": "ELIGIBILITY",
   645	        "effect": {
   646	            "type": "SUPPORT",
   647	            "reason_code": row["reason_code"],
   648	            "covered_purposes": purposes,
   649	        },
   650	        "rule_id": row["rule_id"],
   651	        "priority": 100,
   652	        "on_unknown": "NEEDS_INPUT",
   653	        "source_refs": list(product["source_refs"]),
   654	        "valid_period": {"to": None, "from": NEW_RULE_VALID_FROM},
   655	        "required_facts": _required_facts(when),
   656	        "explanation_key": f"explain.{row['rule_id']}",
   657	        "safety_critical": False,
   658	        "product_version_ids": [product["product_version_id"]],
   659	    }
   660	
   661	
   662	def build_e33_guarantee_rule(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   663	    deposit_min, property_min = _e33_guarantee_bounds(payload)
   664	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   665	    product = products["E33"]
   666	    when = {
   667	        "op": "all",
   668	        "args": [
   669	            {"op": "intersects", "fact": "intent.purposes", "values": ["SECOND_HOME"]},
   670	            {"op": "lt", "fact": DEPOSIT_FACT, "value": deposit_min},
   671	            {"op": "lt", "fact": PROPERTY_FACT, "value": property_min},
   672	        ],
   673	    }
   674	    return {
   675	        "when": when,
   676	        "scope": "PRODUCTS",
   677	        "stage": "HARD_FILTER",
   678	        "effect": {"type": "EXCLUDE", "reason_code": E33_GUARANTEE_REASON},
   679	        "rule_id": E33_GUARANTEE_RULE_ID,
   680	        "priority": 100,
   681	        "on_unknown": "NO_EFFECT",
   682	        "source_refs": list(product["source_refs"]),
   683	        "valid_period": {"to": None, "from": NEW_RULE_VALID_FROM},
   684	        "required_facts": _required_facts(when),
   685	        "explanation_key": f"explain.{E33_GUARANTEE_RULE_ID}",
   686	        "safety_critical": False,
   687	        "product_version_ids": [product["product_version_id"]],
   688	    }
   689	
   690	
   691	def build_employment_sponsor_rule(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   692	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   693	    when = {
   694	        "op": "all",
   695	        "args": [
   696	            {"op": "intersects", "fact": "intent.purposes", "values": ["EMPLOYMENT"]},
   697	            {"op": "eq", "fact": EMPLOYER_IS_INDONESIAN_FACT, "value": False},
   698	        ],
   699	    }
   700	    source_refs: list[str] = []
   701	    for code in EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_PRODUCT_CODES:
   702	        for ref in products[code]["source_refs"]:
   703	            if ref not in source_refs:
   704	                source_refs.append(ref)
   705	    return {
   706	        "when": when,
   707	        "scope": "PRODUCTS",
   708	        "stage": "HARD_FILTER",
   709	        "effect": {"type": "EXCLUDE", "reason_code": EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_REASON},
   710	        "rule_id": EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_RULE_ID,
   711	        "priority": 100,
   712	        "on_unknown": "NO_EFFECT",
   713	        "source_refs": sorted(source_refs),
   714	        "valid_period": {"to": None, "from": NEW_RULE_VALID_FROM},
   715	        "required_facts": _required_facts(when),
   716	        "explanation_key": f"explain.{EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_RULE_ID}",
   717	        "safety_critical": False,
   718	        "product_version_ids": [
   719	            products[code]["product_version_id"] for code in EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_PRODUCT_CODES
   720	        ],
   721	    }
   722	
   723	
   724	def build_new_rules(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   725	    return [
   726	        *(build_support_rule(payload, row) for row in SUPPORT_RULES),
   727	        build_e33_guarantee_rule(payload),
   728	        build_employment_sponsor_rule(payload),
   729	    ]
   730	
   731	
   732	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   733	# Post-conditions.
   734	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   735	
   736	
   737	def assert_only_expected_changes(before: dict[str, Any], after: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   738	    """Fail-closed on both axes: name what MAY move and require universal
   739	    equality for everything else, so a key added in some future sequence is
   740	    guarded automatically rather than escaping an allow-list."""
   741	    if set(after) != set(before):
   742	        _fail(
   743	            "the payload's top-level key set changed "
   744	            f"(added {sorted(set(after) - set(before))}, "
   745	            f"removed {sorted(set(before) - set(after))})"
   746	        )
   747	    for key in set(before) - _IDENTITY_KEYS - {"rules"}:
   748	        if _canon(after.get(key)) != _canon(before.get(key)):
   749	            _fail(f"{key} changed — this fold retires and inserts rules and nothing else")
   750	
   751	    before_rules = _rules_by_id(before)
   752	    after_rules = _rules_by_id(after)
   753	
   754	    missing = set(before_rules) - set(after_rules)
   755	    if missing != set(RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES):
   756	        _fail(f"removed-rule set mismatch: {sorted(missing)} != {sorted(RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES)}")
   757	    added = set(after_rules) - set(before_rules)
   758	    if added != set(NEW_RULE_IDS):
   759	        _fail(f"added-rule set mismatch: {sorted(added)} != {sorted(NEW_RULE_IDS)}")
   760	
   761	    for rule_id, rule in after_rules.items():
   762	        if rule_id in set(NEW_RULE_IDS):
   763	            continue
   764	        if _canon(rule) != _canon(before_rules[rule_id]):
   765	            _fail(
   766	                f"rule {rule_id!r} drifted from seq-20 — this fold edits NO existing "
   767	                "rule, it only retires nine and inserts eleven"
   768	            )
   769	
   770	
   771	def assert_changed_fields_hold_their_expected_values(
   772	    after: dict[str, Any], *, expected_new_rules: list[dict[str, Any]]
   773	) -> None:
   774	    """:func:`assert_only_expected_changes` names what MAY move and excludes it
   775	    from comparison — silent on whether it moved to the RIGHT thing. Pinned
   776	    separately, per the lesson seq-18's review recorded: a guard whose
   777	    fail-closedness holds only while the code above it stays correct is not a
   778	    guard, it is a comment."""
   779	    expected: dict[str, Any] = {
   780	        "sequence": 21,
   781	        "rule_pack_id": str(_rule_pack_id(21)),
   782	        "version": FOLD_VERSION,
   783	        "created_at": FOLD_CREATED_AT,
   784	        "created_by": FOLD_CREATED_BY,
   785	        "previous_payload_sha256": SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256,
   786	        "rollback_of_payload_sha256": None,
   787	    }
   788	    for key, want in expected.items():
   789	        got = after.get(key)
   790	        if got != want:
   791	            _fail(f"{key} is {got!r}, expected {want!r}")
   792	
   793	    rules_by_id = _rules_by_id(after)
   794	    for rule_id in RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES:
   795	        if rule_id in rules_by_id:
   796	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r} should have been retired but is still present")
   797	
   798	    by_id = {rule["rule_id"]: rule for rule in expected_new_rules}
   799	    for rule_id in NEW_RULE_IDS:
   800	        if _canon(rules_by_id.get(rule_id)) != _canon(by_id[rule_id]):
   801	            _fail(f"{rule_id!r} in the output is not the rule this fold built")
   802	
   803	    # Every target product now has exactly one ELIGIBILITY rule, and it reads
   804	    # that product's own qualifying fact. Stated as an OUTPUT census rather
   805	    # than as nine assertions, so a row added to SUPPORT_RULES without a
   806	    # matching product is caught here too.
   807	    for row in SUPPORT_RULES:
   808	        eligibility = _eligibility_rule_ids_for_product(after, row["product_code"])
   809	        if eligibility != {row["rule_id"]}:
   810	            _fail(
   811	                f"product {row['product_code']!r} ends the fold with ELIGIBILITY "
   812	                f"rules {sorted(eligibility)}, expected exactly [{row['rule_id']!r}]"
   813	            )
   814	        facts = rules_by_id[row["rule_id"]]["required_facts"]
   815	        if row["qualifying_fact"] not in facts:
   816	            _fail(
   817	                f"{row['rule_id']!r} does not read its qualifying fact "
   818	                f"{row['qualifying_fact']!r} — a SUPPORT rule keyed on sponsor "
   819	                "category alone is the exact defect this fold exists to cure"
   820	            )
   821	
   822	
   823	def fold(
   824	    seq20: dict[str, Any],
   825	    seq20_signed: dict[str, Any],
   826	    *,
   827	    observed_at: datetime | None = None,
   828	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   829	    """Return the seq-21 payload, or abort loudly.
   830	
   831	    ``seq20_signed`` is the signed seq-20 envelope and is REQUIRED, not
   832	    optional — see :func:`assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact`.
   833	    ``observed_at`` is threaded into that signature check's clock so a test
   834	    pinning it is not left real-clock-dependent.
   835	    """
   836	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonicalize_json(seq20)).hexdigest()
   837	    if digest != SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256:
   838	        _fail(
   839	            "the seq-20 source is not the activated artifact: recomputed JCS "
   840	            f"digest {digest} != {SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256}. Everything below would "
   841	            "otherwise be built on a payload production never ran."
   842	        )
   843	    assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact(seq20_signed, digest, observed_at=observed_at)
   844	    if seq20.get("sequence") != 20:
   845	        _fail(f"expected sequence 20, got {seq20.get('sequence')!r}")
   846	
   847	    inherited_id = seq20.get("rule_pack_id")
   848	    expected_20_id = str(_rule_pack_id(20))
   849	    if inherited_id != expected_20_id:
   850	        _fail(
   851	            f"the seq-20 payload carries rule_pack_id={inherited_id!r}, but the "
   852	            f"uuid5 convention yields {expected_20_id!r} — the anchor is verified, "
   853	            "never assumed."
   854	        )
   855	    collisions = sorted(set(NEW_RULE_IDS) & set(_rules_by_id(seq20)))
   856	    if collisions:
   857	        _fail(f"rule(s) {collisions} already exist in seq-20 — this fold declares them as INSERTs")
   858	
   859	    _assert_target_products_have_no_eligibility_rule(seq20)
   860	    _assert_retired_review_rules_are_dormant(seq20)
   861	    _assert_employment_sponsor_scope_is_coherent(seq20)
   862	    _assert_sponsor_premises_follow_the_catalogue(seq20)
   863	
   864	    new_rules = build_new_rules(seq20)
   865	
   866	    out = json.loads(json.dumps(seq20))
   867	    out["rules"] = [rule for rule in out["rules"] if rule["rule_id"] not in RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES]
   868	    out["rules"].extend(copy.deepcopy(rule) for rule in new_rules)
   869	
   870	    out["sequence"] = 21
   871	    out["rule_pack_id"] = str(_rule_pack_id(21))
   872	    out["version"] = FOLD_VERSION
   873	    out["created_at"] = FOLD_CREATED_AT
   874	    out["created_by"] = FOLD_CREATED_BY
   875	    out["previous_payload_sha256"] = SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   876	    out["rollback_of_payload_sha256"] = None
   877	
   878	    assert_only_expected_changes(seq20, out)
   879	    assert_changed_fields_hold_their_expected_values(out, expected_new_rules=new_rules)
   880	    RulePackPayload.model_validate(out)
   881	    return out
   882	
   883	
   884	def _assert_output_does_not_collide_with_inputs(output: Path, input_paths: dict[str, Path]) -> None:
   885	    """Refuse to write ``--output`` onto any input path, and refuse an output
   886	    path that merely LOOKS like a signed artifact — verbatim the guard
   887	    ``fold_pack_seq20.py`` carries, for the same reason: a copy-pasted flag
   888	    could otherwise overwrite the SIGNED production anchor with unsigned fold
   889	    bytes."""
   890	    resolved_output = output.resolve()
   891	    for flag, path in input_paths.items():
   892	        if resolved_output == path.resolve():
   893	            _fail(
   894	                f"--output {output} resolves to the same file as {flag} "
   895	                f"({path}) — refusing to overwrite an input (or the signed "
   896	                "production anchor) with unsigned fold output"
   897	            )
   898	    if resolved_output.name.endswith(".signed.json"):
   899	        _fail(
   900	            f"--output {output} ends in '.signed.json' — this script only "
   901	            "ever writes an unsigned SOURCE file (see the module docstring); "
   902	            "refusing to write unsigned bytes to a path that looks signed"
   903	        )
   904	
   905	
   906	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, observed_at: datetime | None = None) -> int:
   907	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Fold RulePack seq-21.")
   908	    parser.add_argument("--seq20-source", required=True, type=Path)
   909	    parser.add_argument(
   910	        "--seq20-signed",
   911	        type=Path,
   912	        default=None,
   913	        help=(
   914	            "the signed seq-20 envelope; defaults to the .signed.json sibling "
   915	            "of --seq20-source. Its signature is verified — the anchor is "
   916	            "never taken on the digest constant alone."
   917	        ),
   918	    )
   919	    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, type=Path)
   920	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   921	
   922	    if args.seq20_signed is not None:
   923	        signed_path = args.seq20_signed
   924	    else:
   925	        source_str = str(args.seq20_source)
   926	        if not source_str.endswith(".source.json"):
   927	            _fail(
   928	                f"--seq20-source {source_str!r} does not end in '.source.json' — "
   929	                "cannot derive the signed sibling path; pass --seq20-signed explicitly"
   930	            )
   931	        signed_path = Path(source_str[: -len(".source.json")] + ".signed.json")
   932	    if signed_path == args.seq20_source or not signed_path.exists():
   933	        _fail(f"no signed seq-20 bundle at {signed_path} — pass --seq20-signed")
   934	
   935	    _assert_output_does_not_collide_with_inputs(
   936	        args.output,
   937	        {"--seq20-source": args.seq20_source, "--seq20-signed": signed_path},
   938	    )
   939	
   940	    seq20 = json.loads(args.seq20_source.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   941	    seq20_signed = json.loads(signed_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   942	    seq21 = fold(seq20, seq20_signed, observed_at=observed_at)
   943	    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(seq21, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   944	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonicalize_json(seq21)).hexdigest()
   945	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: wrote {args.output}")
   946	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: seq-21 payload_sha256 = {digest}")
   947	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: retired {sorted(RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES)}")
   948	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: inserted {sorted(NEW_RULE_IDS)}")
   949	    print("fold_pack_seq21: NOT SIGNED, NOT ACTIVATED — see sign_pack.py / activate_pack.py")
   950	    return 0
   951	
   952	
   953	if __name__ == "__main__":
   954	    sys.exit(main())

   260	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reject_visa_activation_insert()
   261	RETURNS trigger
   262	LANGUAGE plpgsql
   263	SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp
   264	AS $$
   265	DECLARE
   266	    pack RECORD;
   267	    head RECORD;
   268	BEGIN
   269	    NEW.activated_by_principal := session_user;
   270	
   271	    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(NEW.environment || NEW.jurisdiction || NEW.decision_domain));
   272	
   273	    SELECT environment, jurisdiction, decision_domain, legal_period, sequence, previous_payload_sha256
   274	        INTO pack
   275	        FROM public.visa_rule_packs
   276	        WHERE id = NEW.rule_pack_id;
   277	    IF NOT FOUND THEN
   278	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'visa activation references unknown rule_pack_id %', NEW.rule_pack_id;
   279	    END IF;
   280	    IF NEW.environment <> pack.environment
   281	       OR NEW.jurisdiction <> pack.jurisdiction
   282	       OR NEW.decision_domain <> pack.decision_domain
   283	       OR NEW.legal_period IS DISTINCT FROM pack.legal_period THEN
   284	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'visa activation scope/legal_period must equal the referenced pack (pack env=% jur=% domain=% legal=%)',
   285	            pack.environment, pack.jurisdiction, pack.decision_domain, pack.legal_period;
   286	    END IF;
   287	
   288	    SELECT p.sequence AS seq, p.payload_sha256 AS hash
   289	        INTO head
   290	        FROM public.visa_ruleset_activations a
   291	        JOIN public.visa_rule_packs p ON p.id = a.rule_pack_id
   292	        WHERE a.environment = NEW.environment
   293	          AND a.jurisdiction = NEW.jurisdiction
   294	          AND a.decision_domain = NEW.decision_domain
   295	          AND a.id <> NEW.id
   296	        ORDER BY p.sequence DESC
   297	        LIMIT 1;
   298	
   299	    IF head IS NULL THEN
   300	        IF pack.previous_payload_sha256 IS NOT NULL THEN
   301	            RAISE EXCEPTION 'visa bootstrap activation must reference a pack with null previous_payload_sha256';
   302	        END IF;
   303	    ELSE
   304	        IF pack.sequence <= head.seq THEN
   305	            RAISE EXCEPTION 'visa activation rollback rejected: pack sequence % <= prior activated sequence %',
   306	                pack.sequence, head.seq;
   307	        END IF;
   308	        IF pack.previous_payload_sha256 IS DISTINCT FROM head.hash THEN
   309	            RAISE EXCEPTION 'visa activation hash chain broken: pack previous_payload_sha256 does not match the current head payload_sha256';
   310	        END IF;
   311	    END IF;
   312	    RETURN NEW;
   313	END;
   314	$$;
   315	
   316	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.reject_visa_activation_mutation()
   317	RETURNS trigger
   318	LANGUAGE plpgsql
   319	SET search_path = pg_catalog, pg_temp
   320	AS $$
   396	    -- 1. Resolve scope + legal_period FROM THE PACK (never from caller args --
   397	    --    prevents scope spoofing).
   398	    SELECT environment, jurisdiction, decision_domain, legal_period
   399	        INTO v_env, v_jur, v_domain, v_legal
   400	        FROM public.visa_rule_packs
   401	        WHERE id = p_rule_pack_id;
   402	    IF NOT FOUND THEN
   403	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'visa_activate_rule_pack: unknown rule_pack_id %', p_rule_pack_id;
   404	    END IF;
   405	
   406	    -- 2. SAME advisory lock key as the insert-guard trigger -- serialize
   407	    --    against any concurrent activation (raw or via this function) for
   408	    --    this triple.
   409	    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(v_env || v_jur || v_domain));
   410	
   411	    -- 3. ONE clock read for the whole supersession (close + insert share
   412	    --    this instant).
   413	    v_now := clock_timestamp();
   414	
   415	    -- 4. Partial-overlap guard (250's PR4/STEP-6 BOUNDARY note, round-4
   416	    --    finding 1): a still-OPEN prior activation whose legal_period
   417	    --    OVERLAPS the new pack's but is NOT fully contained by it would be
   418	    --    orphaned by supersession -- reject. (F5: a legitimate narrowing
   419	    --    correction is a separate, not-yet-built operation -- see 251's
   420	    --    header.)
   421	    SELECT count(*) INTO v_orphan
   422	        FROM public.visa_ruleset_activations a
   423	        WHERE a.environment = v_env AND a.jurisdiction = v_jur AND a.decision_domain = v_domain
   424	          AND upper(a.system_period) IS NULL
   425	          AND a.legal_period && v_legal
   426	          AND NOT (v_legal @> a.legal_period);
   427	    IF v_orphan > 0 THEN
   428	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'visa_activate_rule_pack: partial legal-period overlap would orphan % prior activation(s); refusing (new legal=% must fully cover any overlapping open prior)',
   429	            v_orphan, v_legal;
   430	    END IF;
   431	
   432	    -- 5. Close every still-open prior activation for the triple whose
   433	    --    legal_period is covered by the new one, at v_now (goes through
   434	    --    the mutation-guard trigger's close-open carve-out).
   435	    UPDATE public.visa_ruleset_activations a
   436	        SET system_period = tstzrange(lower(a.system_period), v_now, '[)')
   437	        WHERE a.environment = v_env AND a.jurisdiction = v_jur AND a.decision_domain = v_domain
   438	          AND upper(a.system_period) IS NULL
   439	          AND v_legal @> a.legal_period;
   440	
   441	    -- 6. Insert the new activation open at v_now (goes through the
   442	    --    insert-guard trigger: scope/legal binding +
   443	    --    bootstrap/sequence-monotonicity/hash-chain +
   444	    --    activated_by_principal stamping).
   445	    INSERT INTO public.visa_ruleset_activations
   446	        (rule_pack_id, environment, jurisdiction, decision_domain, legal_period, system_period,
   447	         activated_by, activation_reason)
   448	        VALUES (p_rule_pack_id, v_env, v_jur, v_domain, v_legal,
   449	                tstzrange(v_now, NULL, '[)'), p_activated_by, p_activation_reason)
   450	        RETURNING id INTO v_new_id;

   122	def _build_insert_kwargs(bundle: dict[str, Any], verified: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
   123	    """Map a signed bundle JSON to ``insert_rule_pack`` kwargs.
   124	
   125	    Field shapes mirror the signed artifact written by ``sign_pack.py``:
   126	    top-level ``protected``/``payload``/``payload_sha256``/``signature``
   127	    (the last two as base64url/hex strings).
   128	    """
   129	    protected = bundle["protected"]
   130	    payload = bundle["payload"]
   131	    valid_period = payload["valid_period"]
   132	    legal_from = _parse_iso_utc(valid_period["from"])
   133	    legal_to = _parse_iso_utc(valid_period["to"]) if valid_period.get("to") is not None else None
   134	    return {
   135	        "id": UUID(payload["rule_pack_id"]),
   136	        "environment": payload["environment"],
   137	        "sequence": int(payload["sequence"]),
   138	        "pack_version": payload["version"],
   139	        "engine_contract_version": payload["engine_contract_version"],
   140	        "engine_min_version": payload["engine_min_version"],
   141	        "engine_max_version": payload["engine_max_version"],
   142	        "legal_period": asyncpg.Range(
   143	            lower=legal_from, upper=legal_to, lower_inc=True, upper_inc=False
   144	        ),
   145	        "protected_header": protected,
   146	        "payload": payload,
   147	        "payload_sha256": verified.payload_sha256,
   148	        "previous_payload_sha256": (
   149	            bytes.fromhex(payload["previous_payload_sha256"])
   150	            if payload.get("previous_payload_sha256")
   151	            else None
   152	        ),
   153	        "signature": _b64url_nopad_decode(bundle["signature"]),
   154	        "signing_key_id": protected["kid"],
   155	        "signed_at": _parse_iso_utc(protected["signed_at"]),
   156	    }

   160	    row = await db.fetchrow(
   280	    return False

   283	async def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
   284	    bundle_path = Path(args.signed_bundle)
   285	    bundle = _load_strict_json(bundle_path)
   286	
   287	    trust_store = StaticTrustStore.from_env()
   288	    verified = verify_rule_pack(
   289	        bundle, trust_store=trust_store, observed_at=datetime.now().astimezone()
   290	    )
   291	    logger.info("verified against trust store: kid=%s", bundle["protected"]["kid"])
   292	
   293	    validate_activation(
   294	        verified,
   295	        current_sequence=args.current_sequence,
   296	        current_payload_sha256=(
   297	            bytes.fromhex(args.current_payload_sha256) if args.current_payload_sha256 else None
   298	        ),
   299	        environment=bundle["protected"]["environment"],
   300	        engine_version=args.engine_version,
   301	    )
   302	    logger.info("anti-rollback pre-gate passed")
   303	
   304	    insert_kwargs = _build_insert_kwargs(bundle, verified)
   305	    pack_id: UUID = insert_kwargs["id"]
   306	
   307	    if not args.yes:
   308	        logger.info(
   309	            "dry_run=true would_insert_and_activate=true rule_pack_id=%s sequence=%s "
   310	            "environment=%s payload_sha256=%s actor=%s reason=%s",
   311	            pack_id,
   312	            insert_kwargs["sequence"],
   313	            insert_kwargs["environment"],
   314	            verified.payload_sha256.hex(),
   315	            args.actor,
   316	            args.reason,
   317	        )
   318	        return 0
   319	
   320	    writer_database_url = os.environ.get(args.pack_writer_database_url_env)
   321	    activation_database_url = os.environ.get(args.activation_database_url_env)
   322	    if not writer_database_url:
   323	        raise SystemExit(f"FAIL: ${args.pack_writer_database_url_env} is not set")
   324	    if not activation_database_url:
   325	        raise SystemExit(f"FAIL: ${args.activation_database_url_env} is not set")
   326	
   327	    writer_pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(writer_database_url, min_size=1, max_size=2)
   328	    try:
   329	        activation_pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(activation_database_url, min_size=1, max_size=2)
   330	        try:
   331	            if insert_kwargs["environment"] == "PRODUCTION":
   332	                await _assert_production_separation(writer_pool, activation_pool)
   333	
   334	            existing_hash = await _pack_row_payload_hash(writer_pool, pack_id)
   335	            if existing_hash is not None:
   336	                if existing_hash != verified.payload_sha256:
   337	                    raise SystemExit(
   338	                        f"FAIL: visa_rule_packs already holds {pack_id} with a DIFFERENT "
   339	                        f"payload_sha256 ({existing_hash.hex()[:16]}… vs "
   340	                        f"{verified.payload_sha256.hex()[:16]}…) — packs are immutable"
   430	    signed_at_dt = signed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   431	    assert_created_before_signed(payload.created_at, signed_at_dt)
   432	
   433	    private_key = _load_private_key(key_file)
   434	
   435	    protected: dict = {
   436	        "domain": "balizero.visa-rulepack.v1",
   437	        "alg": "Ed25519",
   438	        "kid": kid,
   439	        "signed_at": utc_isoformat(signed_at_dt),
   440	        "schema_version": "1.0.0",
   441	        "environment": environment,
   442	    }
   443	    payload_dict = payload.model_dump(mode="json", by_alias=True)
   444	
   445	    protected_bytes = canonicalize_json(protected)
   446	    payload_bytes = canonicalize_json(payload_dict)
   447	    payload_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(payload_bytes).hexdigest()
   448	
   449	    signed_bytes = _SIGNING_DOMAIN + b"\x00" + protected_bytes + b"\x00" + payload_bytes
   450	    signature = private_key.sign(signed_bytes)

   692	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   693	    when = {

   714	        "valid_period": {"to": None, "from": NEW_RULE_VALID_FROM},

   736	
   737	def assert_only_expected_changes(before: dict[str, Any], after: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   738	    """Fail-closed on both axes: name what MAY move and require universal
   739	    equality for everything else, so a key added in some future sequence is
   740	    guarded automatically rather than escaping an allow-list."""
   741	    if set(after) != set(before):
   742	        _fail(
   743	            "the payload's top-level key set changed "
   744	            f"(added {sorted(set(after) - set(before))}, "
   745	            f"removed {sorted(set(before) - set(after))})"
   746	        )
   747	    for key in set(before) - _IDENTITY_KEYS - {"rules"}:
   748	        if _canon(after.get(key)) != _canon(before.get(key)):
   749	            _fail(f"{key} changed — this fold retires and inserts rules and nothing else")
   750	
   751	    before_rules = _rules_by_id(before)
   752	    after_rules = _rules_by_id(after)
   753	
   754	    missing = set(before_rules) - set(after_rules)
   755	    if missing != set(RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES):
   756	        _fail(f"removed-rule set mismatch: {sorted(missing)} != {sorted(RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES)}")
   757	    added = set(after_rules) - set(before_rules)
   758	    if added != set(NEW_RULE_IDS):
   759	        _fail(f"added-rule set mismatch: {sorted(added)} != {sorted(NEW_RULE_IDS)}")
   760	
   761	    for rule_id, rule in after_rules.items():
   762	        if rule_id in set(NEW_RULE_IDS):
   763	            continue
   764	        if _canon(rule) != _canon(before_rules[rule_id]):
   765	            _fail(
   766	                f"rule {rule_id!r} drifted from seq-20 — this fold edits NO existing "
   767	                "rule, it only retires nine and inserts eleven"
   768	            )
   769	
   770	
   771	def assert_changed_fields_hold_their_expected_values(
   772	    after: dict[str, Any], *, expected_new_rules: list[dict[str, Any]]
   773	) -> None:
   774	    """:func:`assert_only_expected_changes` names what MAY move and excludes it
   775	    from comparison — silent on whether it moved to the RIGHT thing. Pinned
   776	    separately, per the lesson seq-18's review recorded: a guard whose
   777	    fail-closedness holds only while the code above it stays correct is not a
   778	    guard, it is a comment."""
   779	    expected: dict[str, Any] = {
   780	        "sequence": 21,
   781	        "rule_pack_id": str(_rule_pack_id(21)),
   782	        "version": FOLD_VERSION,
   783	        "created_at": FOLD_CREATED_AT,
   784	        "created_by": FOLD_CREATED_BY,
   785	        "previous_payload_sha256": SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256,
   786	        "rollback_of_payload_sha256": None,
   787	    }
   788	    for key, want in expected.items():
   789	        got = after.get(key)
   790	        if got != want:
   791	            _fail(f"{key} is {got!r}, expected {want!r}")
   792	
   793	    rules_by_id = _rules_by_id(after)
   794	    for rule_id in RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES:
   795	        if rule_id in rules_by_id:
   796	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r} should have been retired but is still present")
   797	
   798	    by_id = {rule["rule_id"]: rule for rule in expected_new_rules}
   799	    for rule_id in NEW_RULE_IDS:
   800	        if _canon(rules_by_id.get(rule_id)) != _canon(by_id[rule_id]):
   801	            _fail(f"{rule_id!r} in the output is not the rule this fold built")
   802	
   803	    # Every target product now has exactly one ELIGIBILITY rule, and it reads
   804	    # that product's own qualifying fact. Stated as an OUTPUT census rather
   805	    # than as nine assertions, so a row added to SUPPORT_RULES without a
   806	    # matching product is caught here too.
   807	    for row in SUPPORT_RULES:
   808	        eligibility = _eligibility_rule_ids_for_product(after, row["product_code"])
   809	        if eligibility != {row["rule_id"]}:
   810	            _fail(
   811	                f"product {row['product_code']!r} ends the fold with ELIGIBILITY "
   812	                f"rules {sorted(eligibility)}, expected exactly [{row['rule_id']!r}]"
   813	            )
   814	        facts = rules_by_id[row["rule_id"]]["required_facts"]
   815	        if row["qualifying_fact"] not in facts:
   816	            _fail(
   817	                f"{row['rule_id']!r} does not read its qualifying fact "
   818	                f"{row['qualifying_fact']!r} — a SUPPORT rule keyed on sponsor "
   819	                "category alone is the exact defect this fold exists to cure"
   820	            )
   821	
   822	
   823	def fold(
   824	    seq20: dict[str, Any],
   825	    seq20_signed: dict[str, Any],
   826	    *,
   827	    observed_at: datetime | None = None,
   828	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   829	    """Return the seq-21 payload, or abort loudly.
   830	
   831	    ``seq20_signed`` is the signed seq-20 envelope and is REQUIRED, not
   832	    optional — see :func:`assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact`.
   833	    ``observed_at`` is threaded into that signature check's clock so a test
   834	    pinning it is not left real-clock-dependent.
   835	    """
   836	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonicalize_json(seq20)).hexdigest()
   837	    if digest != SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256:
   838	        _fail(
   839	            "the seq-20 source is not the activated artifact: recomputed JCS "
   840	            f"digest {digest} != {SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256}. Everything below would "
   841	            "otherwise be built on a payload production never ran."
   842	        )
   843	    assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact(seq20_signed, digest, observed_at=observed_at)
   844	    if seq20.get("sequence") != 20:
   845	        _fail(f"expected sequence 20, got {seq20.get('sequence')!r}")
   846	
   847	    inherited_id = seq20.get("rule_pack_id")
   848	    expected_20_id = str(_rule_pack_id(20))
   849	    if inherited_id != expected_20_id:
   850	        _fail(
   851	            f"the seq-20 payload carries rule_pack_id={inherited_id!r}, but the "
   852	            f"uuid5 convention yields {expected_20_id!r} — the anchor is verified, "
   853	            "never assumed."
   854	        )
   855	    collisions = sorted(set(NEW_RULE_IDS) & set(_rules_by_id(seq20)))
   856	    if collisions:
   857	        _fail(f"rule(s) {collisions} already exist in seq-20 — this fold declares them as INSERTs")
   858	
   859	    _assert_target_products_have_no_eligibility_rule(seq20)
   860	    _assert_retired_review_rules_are_dormant(seq20)
   861	    _assert_employment_sponsor_scope_is_coherent(seq20)
   862	    _assert_sponsor_premises_follow_the_catalogue(seq20)
   863	
   864	    new_rules = build_new_rules(seq20)
   865	
   866	    out = json.loads(json.dumps(seq20))
   867	    out["rules"] = [rule for rule in out["rules"] if rule["rule_id"] not in RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES]
   868	    out["rules"].extend(copy.deepcopy(rule) for rule in new_rules)
   869	
   870	    out["sequence"] = 21
   871	    out["rule_pack_id"] = str(_rule_pack_id(21))
   872	    out["version"] = FOLD_VERSION
   873	    out["created_at"] = FOLD_CREATED_AT
   874	    out["created_by"] = FOLD_CREATED_BY
   875	    out["previous_payload_sha256"] = SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   876	    out["rollback_of_payload_sha256"] = None
   877	
   878	    assert_only_expected_changes(seq20, out)
   879	    assert_changed_fields_hold_their_expected_values(out, expected_new_rules=new_rules)
   880	    RulePackPayload.model_validate(out)
   881	    return out
   882	
   883	
   884	def _assert_output_does_not_collide_with_inputs(output: Path, input_paths: dict[str, Path]) -> None:
   885	    """Refuse to write ``--output`` onto any input path, and refuse an output
   886	    path that merely LOOKS like a signed artifact — verbatim the guard
   887	    ``fold_pack_seq20.py`` carries, for the same reason: a copy-pasted flag
   888	    could otherwise overwrite the SIGNED production anchor with unsigned fold
   889	    bytes."""
   890	    resolved_output = output.resolve()
   891	    for flag, path in input_paths.items():
   892	        if resolved_output == path.resolve():
   893	            _fail(
   894	                f"--output {output} resolves to the same file as {flag} "
   895	                f"({path}) — refusing to overwrite an input (or the signed "
   896	                "production anchor) with unsigned fold output"
   897	            )
   898	    if resolved_output.name.endswith(".signed.json"):
   899	        _fail(
   900	            f"--output {output} ends in '.signed.json' — this script only "
   901	            "ever writes an unsigned SOURCE file (see the module docstring); "
   902	            "refusing to write unsigned bytes to a path that looks signed"
   903	        )
   904	
   905	
   906	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, observed_at: datetime | None = None) -> int:
   907	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Fold RulePack seq-21.")
   908	    parser.add_argument("--seq20-source", required=True, type=Path)
   909	    parser.add_argument(
   910	        "--seq20-signed",
   911	        type=Path,
   912	        default=None,
   913	        help=(
   914	            "the signed seq-20 envelope; defaults to the .signed.json sibling "
   915	            "of --seq20-source. Its signature is verified — the anchor is "
   916	            "never taken on the digest constant alone."
   917	        ),
   918	    )
   919	    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, type=Path)
   920	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

   348	def _rule_pack_id(sequence: int) -> uuid.UUID:
   349	    return uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_URL, f"{_RULE_PACK_ID_URL_PREFIX}{sequence}")

   351	
   352	def _fail(message: str) -> None:
   353	    raise SystemExit(f"fold_pack_seq21: {message}")
   354	
   355	
   356	def _canon(obj: Any) -> str:
   357	    return json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
   358	
   359	
   360	def _nodes(node: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   361	    """Every dict in a condition tree, in a deterministic pre-order."""
   362	    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   363	    if isinstance(node, dict):
   364	        out.append(node)
   365	        for value in node.values():
   366	            out.extend(_nodes(value))
   367	    elif isinstance(node, list):
   368	        for value in node:
   369	            out.extend(_nodes(value))
   370	    return out
   371	
   372	
   373	def _rules_by_id(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   374	    return {rule["rule_id"]: rule for rule in payload["rules"]}
   375	
   376	
   377	def _products_by_code(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   378	    return {product["product_code"]: product for product in payload["products"]}
   379	
   380	
   381	def _required_facts(when: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
   382	    """The sorted, de-duplicated fact paths a condition tree reads.
   383	
   384	    Derived from the built condition rather than declared beside it, because
   385	    the compiler's ``_check_required_facts_match_condition`` compares exactly
   386	    these two and a hand-written list is one edit away from disagreeing.
   387	    """
   388	    facts = {node["fact"] for node in _nodes(when) if isinstance(node.get("fact"), str)}
   389	    return sorted(facts)
   390	
   391	
   392	def assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact(
   393	    signed_envelope: dict[str, Any],
   394	    digest: str,
   395	    *,
   396	    observed_at: datetime | None = None,
   397	) -> None:
   398	    """Require the chain anchor to be a SIGNED seq-20 pack, not just a digest.
   399	
   400	    Same shape as ``fold_pack_seq20.assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact``
   401	    — that one is pinned to sequence 19 by its own final check, so it is
   402	    reproduced here pinned to 20 rather than parameterised after the fact.
   403	    """
   404	    try:
   405	        trust_store = StaticTrustStore.from_env()
   406	    except RulePackVerificationError as exc:
   407	        _fail(
   408	            f"cannot verify the seq-20 anchor's signature: {exc}. Export the "
   409	            "production trust store (the public key, e.g. "
   410	            'VISA_ENGINE_TRUST_STORE_KEYS_JSON=\'[{"kid": "prod-2026-07-1", ...}]\') '
   411	            "and re-run — the anchor is never taken on a digest constant alone."
   412	        )
   413	    try:
   414	        verified = verify_rule_pack(
   415	            signed_envelope,
   416	            trust_store=trust_store,
   417	            observed_at=observed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
   418	        )
   419	    except RulePackVerificationError as exc:
   420	        _fail(f"the seq-20 signed bundle does not verify: {exc}")
   421	
   422	    signed_digest = verified.payload_sha256.hex()
   423	    if signed_digest != digest:
   424	        _fail(
   425	            f"the seq-20 SOURCE digest {digest} is not the digest of the signed "
   426	            f"seq-20 artifact ({signed_digest}) — the source on disk and the "
   427	            "artifact production verifies are two different payloads."
   428	        )
   429	    if verified.pack.payload.sequence != 20:
   430	        _fail(
   431	            "the signed bundle handed in as the seq-20 anchor carries sequence "
   432	            f"{verified.pack.payload.sequence}"
   433	        )
   434	
   435	
   436	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   437	# Pre-flight census checks — each edit's premise, verified against the base
   438	# pack rather than trusted from the docstring.
   439	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   440	
   441	
   442	def _eligibility_rule_ids_for_product(payload: dict[str, Any], product_code: str) -> set[str]:
   443	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   444	    if product_code not in products:
   445	        _fail(f"product {product_code!r} is not in the catalogue")
   446	    version_id = products[product_code]["product_version_id"]
   447	    return {
   448	        rule["rule_id"]
   449	        for rule in payload["rules"]
   450	        if rule["stage"] == "ELIGIBILITY" and version_id in rule.get("product_version_ids", [])
   451	    }
   452	
   453	
   454	def _assert_target_products_have_no_eligibility_rule(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   455	    """The nine products this fold unblocks must currently carry ZERO
   456	    ELIGIBILITY rules.
   457	
   458	    That is the whole claim — "structurally unrecommendable, because
   459	    ``declared_coverage`` is empty". If a base pack has meanwhile given one of
   460	    them a SUPPORT rule, this fold would be adding a SECOND, differently-keyed
   461	    route to the same product without anyone comparing the two; refuse instead.
   462	    """
   463	    for code in TARGET_PRODUCT_CODES:
   464	        existing = _eligibility_rule_ids_for_product(payload, code)
   465	        if existing:
   466	            _fail(
   467	                f"product {code!r} already carries ELIGIBILITY rule(s) "
   468	                f"{sorted(existing)} in the base pack — this fold's premise is "
   469	                "that the nine target products have none, so it refuses rather "
   470	                "than adding a second, unreconciled route to the same product"
   471	            )
   472	
   473	
   474	def _assert_retired_review_rules_are_dormant(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   475	    """Every rule this fold retires must be a REQUIRE_REVIEW rule gated on
   476	    ``intent.requested_product_code``, scoped to the product it is declared
   477	    against.
   478	
   479	    The retirement's justification is that the rule is dormant BY
   480	    CONSTRUCTION (``fact-mapper.ts`` never sets that fact) and that its review
   481	    question is now a fact the new SUPPORT rule tests. If a future base pack
   482	    gives one of them a condition that can actually fire from the browser,
   483	    retiring it would drop a live gate — so this refuses rather than deleting.
   484	    """
   485	    rules_by_id = _rules_by_id(payload)
   486	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   487	    for rule_id, product_code in RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES.items():
   488	        rule = rules_by_id.get(rule_id)
   489	        if rule is None:
   490	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r} is not in the base pack — the edit set is stale")
   491	        if rule["effect"]["type"] != "REQUIRE_REVIEW":
   492	            _fail(
   493	                f"{rule_id!r} is a {rule['effect']['type']} rule, not REQUIRE_REVIEW — "
   494	                "refusing to retire it under an edit that only claims to retire "
   495	                "dormant review gates"
   496	            )
   497	        if REQUESTED_PRODUCT_FACT not in (rule.get("required_facts") or []):
   498	            _fail(
   499	                f"{rule_id!r} no longer reads {REQUESTED_PRODUCT_FACT} — it can now "
   500	                "fire from the browser, so retiring it would drop a live gate"
   501	            )
   502	        version_id = products[product_code]["product_version_id"]
   503	        if version_id not in rule.get("product_version_ids", []):
   504	            _fail(
   505	                f"{rule_id!r} is not scoped to {product_code} — the declared "
   506	                "rule/product pairing is stale"
   507	            )
   508	
   509	
   510	def _bound_nodes(rule: dict[str, Any], fact: str, op: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   511	    return [
   512	        node
   513	        for node in _nodes(rule.get("when"))
   514	        if node.get("fact") == fact and node.get("op") == op
   515	    ]
   516	
   517	
   518	def _e33_guarantee_bounds(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:
   519	    """``(deposit_usd_minimum, property_usd_minimum)``, READ from the two E33
   520	    SUPPORT rules that already encode them.
   521	
   522	    The named-cause EXCLUDE this fold adds says "below the threshold". If the
   523	    number it uses were typed here it could drift from the number the SUPPORT
   524	    rule uses, and the pack would simultaneously deny a product for being
   525	    below X and grant it at Y. Extracting both makes that impossible and turns
   526	    a moved bound into a fold abort.
   527	    """
   528	    rules_by_id = _rules_by_id(payload)
   529	    bounds: list[int] = []
   530	    for rule_id, fact in (
   531	        (E33_DEPOSIT_DONOR_RULE_ID, DEPOSIT_FACT),
   532	        (E33_PROPERTY_DONOR_RULE_ID, PROPERTY_FACT),
   533	    ):
   534	        rule = rules_by_id.get(rule_id)
   535	        if rule is None:
   536	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r} is missing — cannot derive the E33 guarantee bound")
   537	        nodes = _bound_nodes(rule, fact, "gte")
   538	        if len(nodes) != 1:
   539	            _fail(
   540	                f"rule {rule_id!r} carries {len(nodes)} '{fact} gte' bounds; exactly "
   541	                "one is required to mirror it unambiguously"
   542	            )
   543	        value = nodes[0]["value"]
   544	        if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value <= 0:
   545	            _fail(f"rule {rule_id!r}'s '{fact} gte' bound is {value!r}, not a positive integer")
   546	        bounds.append(value)
   547	    return bounds[0], bounds[1]
   548	
   549	
   550	def _assert_employment_sponsor_scope_is_coherent(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   551	    """Every product the employment EXCLUDE is scoped to must declare
   552	    EMPLOYMENT among its ``covered_purposes``.
   553	
   554	    A hard filter conditioned on ``intent.purposes intersects [EMPLOYMENT]``
   555	    that is scoped to a product which cannot cover EMPLOYMENT in the first
   556	    place is dead weight at best and a misleading exclusion reason at worst.
   557	    """
   558	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   559	    for code in EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_PRODUCT_CODES:
   560	        if code not in products:
   561	            _fail(
   562	                f"product {code!r} is not in the catalogue — cannot scope {EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_RULE_ID}"
   563	            )
   564	        covered = products[code].get("covered_purposes") or []
   565	        if "EMPLOYMENT" not in covered:
   566	            _fail(
   567	                f"product {code!r} does not cover EMPLOYMENT ({covered}) — "
   568	                f"{EMPLOYMENT_SPONSOR_RULE_ID} would be scoped to a product its own "
   569	                "condition can never concern"
   570	            )
   571	    if EMPLOYER_IS_INDONESIAN_FACT not in {
   572	        node.get("fact") for rule in payload["rules"] for node in _nodes(rule.get("when"))
   573	    }:
   574	        _fail(
   575	            f"no rule in the base pack reads {EMPLOYER_IS_INDONESIAN_FACT} — the "
   576	            "fact this exclusion turns on is not one the interview establishes, "
   577	            "so the new rule could never fire"
   578	        )
   579	
   580	
   581	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   582	# The edits themselves.
   583	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   584	
   585	
   586	def _sponsor_premise_values(row: dict[str, Any]) -> set[str] | None:
   587	    for node in row["premises"]:
   588	        if node.get("fact") != "sponsor.type":
   589	            continue
   590	        if node["op"] == "eq":
   591	            return {node["value"]}
   592	        if node["op"] == "in":
   593	            return set(node["values"])
   594	        _fail(f"{row['rule_id']}: unsupported sponsor.type premise op {node['op']!r}")
   595	    return None
   596	
   597	
   598	def _assert_sponsor_premises_follow_the_catalogue(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   599	    """A SUPPORT rule may only admit sponsor categories its product's catalogue
   600	    record names. A wider premise would RECOMMEND a product on a sponsor answer
   601	    the product itself says it does not take."""
   602	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   603	    for row in SUPPORT_RULES:
   604	        admitted = _sponsor_premise_values(row)
   605	        if admitted is None:
   606	            continue
   607	        catalogue = set(products[row["product_code"]]["sponsor_types"])
   608	        if not admitted <= catalogue:
   609	            _fail(
   610	                f"{row['rule_id']} admits sponsor.type {sorted(admitted - catalogue)} that "
   611	                f"{row['product_code']}'s catalogue sponsor_types {sorted(catalogue)} does not name"
   612	            )
   613	
   614

   617	    citations DERIVED from the product catalogue rather than transcribed."""
   618	    products = _products_by_code(payload)
   619	    code = row["product_code"]
   620	    if code not in products:

+  /**
+   * The highest-sequence SIGNED pack's payload. Only a signed pack can be in
+   * force, so this is the upper bound of what production can emit today —
+   * while `latestProductionPackFile()` is the upper bound of what it will
+   * emit next. W-VO-S21 (2026-09-13) is why both are read: an unsigned
+   * seq-21 source retires eight review codes that the signed, live seq-20
+   * still carries. Reading only the source would call their copy "stale"
+   * and delete it from the live pack's sheet; reading only the signed pack
+   * would miss a code the next pack adds. The union keeps every code either
+   * pack can emit covered — and the day seq-21's signed bundle lands, the
+   * eight retired keys stop being in the union and the stale-key test below
+   * names them, which is the moment their copy is allowed to go.
+   */
+  function latestSignedProductionPayload(): {
+    rules?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
+  } {
+    let best: {
+      payload: { rules?: Array<Record<string, unknown>> };
+      sequence: number;
+    } | null = null;
+    for (const name of fs.readdirSync(PACKS_DIR)) {
+      if (!/^rulepack-prod-\d+\.signed\.json$/.test(name)) continue;
+      const envelope = JSON.parse(
+        fs.readFileSync(path.join(PACKS_DIR, name), "utf-8"),
+      ) as {
+        payload?: {
+          sequence?: unknown;
+          rules?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
+        };
+      };
+      const payload = envelope.payload;
+      if (!payload || typeof payload.sequence !== "number") continue;
+      if (best === null || payload.sequence > best.sequence) {
+        best = { payload, sequence: payload.sequence };
+      }
+    }
+    if (best === null) {
+      throw new Error(
+        `no signed pack under ${PACKS_DIR} had a numeric sequence`,
+      );
+    }
+    return best.payload;
+  }

   return (
     SUPPORT_REASON_COPY[code] ??
     text(`Verified reason: ${code}`, `Alasan terverifikasi: ${code}`)
   );
 }

+/**
+ * The no-path reason codes whose sentence is reconstructed from the

+ * walks through the engine's generic OPERATIONAL fallback; seq-21 reaches

+  // fact-derived sentences below have to follow the rename or they would

+  // why it was written in the first place (#6441). The static entry in
+  // SUPPORT_REASON_COPY remains the fallback for every walk whose facts do
+  // not reconstruct the number.
+  const message = FACT_DERIVED_NO_PATH_CODES.has(code)
+    ? (secondHomeBelowThresholdReason(facts ?? {}) ??
+      paidActivityWithoutIndonesianPayerReason(facts ?? {}) ??
+      reasonMessage(code))
+    : reasonMessage(code);
   return {
     code,
     message,
     sourceIds: sourceIds.filter((id) => trustedIds.has(id)),
   };
 }

   738	    """Fail-closed on both axes: name what MAY move and require universal

   823	def fold(
   824	    seq20: dict[str, Any],
   825	    seq20_signed: dict[str, Any],
   826	    *,
   827	    observed_at: datetime | None = None,
   828	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   829	    """Return the seq-21 payload, or abort loudly.
   830	
   831	    ``seq20_signed`` is the signed seq-20 envelope and is REQUIRED, not
   832	    optional — see :func:`assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact`.
   833	    ``observed_at`` is threaded into that signature check's clock so a test
   834	    pinning it is not left real-clock-dependent.
   835	    """
   836	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonicalize_json(seq20)).hexdigest()
   837	    if digest != SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256:
   838	        _fail(
   839	            "the seq-20 source is not the activated artifact: recomputed JCS "
   840	            f"digest {digest} != {SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256}. Everything below would "
   841	            "otherwise be built on a payload production never ran."
   842	        )
   843	    assert_anchor_is_a_verified_signed_artifact(seq20_signed, digest, observed_at=observed_at)
   844	    if seq20.get("sequence") != 20:
   845	        _fail(f"expected sequence 20, got {seq20.get('sequence')!r}")
   846	
   847	    inherited_id = seq20.get("rule_pack_id")
   848	    expected_20_id = str(_rule_pack_id(20))
   849	    if inherited_id != expected_20_id:
   850	        _fail(
   851	            f"the seq-20 payload carries rule_pack_id={inherited_id!r}, but the "
   852	            f"uuid5 convention yields {expected_20_id!r} — the anchor is verified, "
   853	            "never assumed."
   854	        )
   855	    collisions = sorted(set(NEW_RULE_IDS) & set(_rules_by_id(seq20)))
   856	    if collisions:
   857	        _fail(f"rule(s) {collisions} already exist in seq-20 — this fold declares them as INSERTs")
   858	
   859	    _assert_target_products_have_no_eligibility_rule(seq20)
   860	    _assert_retired_review_rules_are_dormant(seq20)
   861	    _assert_employment_sponsor_scope_is_coherent(seq20)
   862	    _assert_sponsor_premises_follow_the_catalogue(seq20)
   863	
   864	    new_rules = build_new_rules(seq20)
   865	
   866	    out = json.loads(json.dumps(seq20))
   867	    out["rules"] = [rule for rule in out["rules"] if rule["rule_id"] not in RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES]
   868	    out["rules"].extend(copy.deepcopy(rule) for rule in new_rules)
   869	
   870	    out["sequence"] = 21
   871	    out["rule_pack_id"] = str(_rule_pack_id(21))
   872	    out["version"] = FOLD_VERSION
   873	    out["created_at"] = FOLD_CREATED_AT
   874	    out["created_by"] = FOLD_CREATED_BY
   875	    out["previous_payload_sha256"] = SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   876	    out["rollback_of_payload_sha256"] = None
   877

   879	    assert_changed_fields_hold_their_expected_values(out, expected_new_rules=new_rules)
   880	    RulePackPayload.model_validate(out)
   881	    return out

   884	def _assert_output_does_not_collide_with_inputs(output: Path, input_paths: dict[str, Path]) -> None:
   885	    """Refuse to write ``--output`` onto any input path, and refuse an output
   886	    path that merely LOOKS like a signed artifact — verbatim the guard
   887	    ``fold_pack_seq20.py`` carries, for the same reason: a copy-pasted flag
   888	    could otherwise overwrite the SIGNED production anchor with unsigned fold
   889	    bytes."""
   890	    resolved_output = output.resolve()
   891	    for flag, path in input_paths.items():
   892	        if resolved_output == path.resolve():
   893	            _fail(
   894	                f"--output {output} resolves to the same file as {flag} "
   895	                f"({path}) — refusing to overwrite an input (or the signed "
   896	                "production anchor) with unsigned fold output"
   897	            )
   898	    if resolved_output.name.endswith(".signed.json"):
   899	        _fail(
   900	            f"--output {output} ends in '.signed.json' — this script only "
   901	            "ever writes an unsigned SOURCE file (see the module docstring); "
   902	            "refusing to write unsigned bytes to a path that looks signed"
   903	        )
   904	
   905	
   906	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, observed_at: datetime | None = None) -> int:
   907	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Fold RulePack seq-21.")
   908	    parser.add_argument("--seq20-source", required=True, type=Path)
   909	    parser.add_argument(
   910	        "--seq20-signed",
   911	        type=Path,
   912	        default=None,
   913	        help=(
   914	            "the signed seq-20 envelope; defaults to the .signed.json sibling "
   915	            "of --seq20-source. Its signature is verified — the anchor is "
   916	            "never taken on the digest constant alone."
   917	        ),
   918	    )
   919	    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, type=Path)
   920	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   921	
   922	    if args.seq20_signed is not None:
   923	        signed_path = args.seq20_signed
   924	    else:
   925	        source_str = str(args.seq20_source)
   926	        if not source_str.endswith(".source.json"):
   927	            _fail(
   928	                f"--seq20-source {source_str!r} does not end in '.source.json' — "
   929	                "cannot derive the signed sibling path; pass --seq20-signed explicitly"
   930	            )
   931	        signed_path = Path(source_str[: -len(".source.json")] + ".signed.json")
   932	    if signed_path == args.seq20_source or not signed_path.exists():
   933	        _fail(f"no signed seq-20 bundle at {signed_path} — pass --seq20-signed")
   934	
   935	    _assert_output_does_not_collide_with_inputs(
   936	        args.output,
   937	        {"--seq20-source": args.seq20_source, "--seq20-signed": signed_path},
   938	    )
   939	
   940	    seq20 = json.loads(args.seq20_source.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   941	    seq20_signed = json.loads(signed_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   942	    seq21 = fold(seq20, seq20_signed, observed_at=observed_at)
   943	    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(seq21, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   944	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonicalize_json(seq21)).hexdigest()
   945	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: wrote {args.output}")
   946	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: seq-21 payload_sha256 = {digest}")
   947	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: retired {sorted(RETIRED_REVIEW_RULES)}")
   948	    print(f"fold_pack_seq21: inserted {sorted(NEW_RULE_IDS)}")
   949	    print("fold_pack_seq21: NOT SIGNED, NOT ACTIVATED — see sign_pack.py / activate_pack.py")
   950	    return 0
   951	
   952	
   953	if __name__ == "__main__":
   954	    sys.exit(main())
