   362	class TestFoldIntegrity:
   363	    def test_fold_is_deterministic_and_matches_disk(
   364	        self,
   365	        seq20_source: dict[str, Any],
   366	        seq20_signed: dict[str, Any],
   367	        seq21_source: dict[str, Any],
   368	        prod_trust_store_env: None,
   369	    ) -> None:
   370	        assert fold(seq20_source, seq20_signed, observed_at=OBSERVED_AT) == seq21_source
   371	
   372	    def test_the_cli_reproduces_the_bytes_the_owner_signs(
   373	        self,
   374	        tmp_path: Path,
   375	        seq21_source: dict[str, Any],
   376	        prod_trust_store_env: None,
   377	    ) -> None:
   378	        """Byte-level reproducibility, stated at the level that is signed.
   379	
   380	        The committed file is NOT the writer's raw output: the repo's
   381	        pre-commit hook runs prettier over every staged ``*.json`` (root
   382	        ``package.json`` lint-staged), which reflows arrays — seq-19 and
   383	        seq-20 carry the same reflow. What ``sign_pack.py`` signs is JCS over
   384	        the parsed payload, so the byte contract a third party can re-execute
   385	        is: run the CLI, canonicalize its file, and get EXACTLY the canonical
   386	        bytes of the committed file, whose sha256 is the digest the owner
   387	        signs. Comparing parsed dicts (the test above) would not catch a
   388	        float/int or key-encoding drift that JCS would sign differently.
   389	
   390	        The FILE bytes are reproducible too, one step further out: the CLI's
   391	        output piped through ``prettier --stdin-filepath <this pack's path>``
   392	        (prettier 3.9.6, repo root) is sha256-identical to the committed file
   393	        — measured 2026-09-14, recorded in the evidence pack rather than
   394	        asserted here, because this suite does not run node.
   395	        """
   396	        output = tmp_path / "rulepack-prod-021.source.json"
   397	        assert (
   398	            main(
   399	                [
   400	                    "--seq20-source",
   401	                    str(_SEQ20_SOURCE_PATH),
   402	                    "--seq20-signed",
   403	                    str(_SEQ20_SIGNED_PATH),
   404	                    "--output",
   405	                    str(output),
   406	                ],
   407	                observed_at=OBSERVED_AT,
   408	            )
   409	            == 0
   410	        )
   411	        produced = canonicalize_json(json.loads(output.read_bytes()))
   412	        committed = canonicalize_json(seq21_source)
   413	        assert produced == committed
   414	        assert hashlib.sha256(produced).hexdigest() == SEQ21_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   415	        # sign_pack.py canonicalizes the TYPED payload
   416	        # (``RulePackPayload.model_dump(mode="json", by_alias=True)``), not the
   417	        # raw JSON: the digest above is only the signed one if both agree.
   418	        typed = RulePackPayload.model_validate(seq21_source).model_dump(mode="json", by_alias=True)
   419	        assert canonicalize_json(typed) == committed
   420	
   505	class TestIdentity:
   506	    def test_sequence_is_21(self, seq21_source: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   507	        assert seq21_source["sequence"] == 21
   508	
   509	    def test_rule_pack_id_follows_the_uuid5_convention(self, seq21_source: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   510	        assert seq21_source["rule_pack_id"] == str(_rule_pack_id(21))
   511	
   512	    def test_chain_anchor_is_the_measured_seq20_digest(
   513	        self, seq20_source: dict[str, Any], seq21_source: dict[str, Any]
   514	    ) -> None:
   515	        """The anchor is re-derived from the seq-20 bytes on disk, never taken
   516	        from the constant alone."""
   517	        recomputed = hashlib.sha256(canonicalize_json(seq20_source)).hexdigest()
   518	        assert recomputed == SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   519	        assert seq21_source["previous_payload_sha256"] == SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   520	
   521	    def test_chain_anchor_is_also_the_signed_artifacts_own_digest(
   522	        self, seq20_signed: dict[str, Any]
   523	    ) -> None:
   524	        assert seq20_signed["payload_sha256"] == SEQ20_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   525	
   526	    def test_payload_digest_survives_reformatting_of_the_file(
   527	        self, seq21_source: dict[str, Any]
   528	    ) -> None:
   529	        """The pack's identity is JCS over the PARSED document, never the file
   530	        bytes, so the repo's prettier gate can reshape the file freely. The
   531	        constant is the digest the owner signs; it was measured BEFORE the
   532	        prettier pass and re-measured after it, unchanged."""
   533	        digest = hashlib.sha256(canonicalize_json(seq21_source)).hexdigest()
   534	        assert digest == SEQ21_PAYLOAD_SHA256
   535	
   536	    def test_pack_legal_period_is_the_incumbents(
   537	        self, seq21_source: dict[str, Any], seq20_source: dict[str, Any]
   538	    ) -> None:
   539	        """The pack's ``valid_period`` does not move, as in every fold since
   540	        seq-13: it becomes the activation's ``legal_period``, and "not before
   541	        the signature" is carried by the NEW rules' own validity instead."""
   542	        assert seq21_source["valid_period"] == seq20_source["valid_period"]
   543	
   544	    @staticmethod
   545	    def _activation_would_be_refused(new: dict[str, Any], incumbent: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   546	        """``visa_activate_rule_pack`` step 4 (migration 253), restated: an
   547	        open prior activation whose legal period OVERLAPS the new one without
   548	        being fully covered by it is an orphan, and the function refuses.
   549	        Both periods here are ``[from, to)`` with ``to = None`` meaning open."""
   550	
   551	        def bounds(period: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[datetime, datetime]:
   552	            low = datetime.fromisoformat(period["from"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   553	            high = (
   554	                datetime.max.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   555	                if period["to"] is None
   556	                else datetime.fromisoformat(period["to"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   557	            )
   558	            return low, high
   559	
   560	        new_low, new_high = bounds(new["valid_period"])
   561	        old_low, old_high = bounds(incumbent["valid_period"])
   562	        overlaps = new_low < old_high and old_low < new_high
   563	        covers = new_low <= old_low and old_high <= new_high
   564	        return overlaps and not covers
   565	
   566	    def test_the_activation_writer_would_accept_it_over_seq20(

   576	        2026-07-25) the DB would have inserted the immutable pack row and then
   577	        refused the activation — sequence 21 burned at the ``--yes`` step."""
   578	        mutated = copy.deepcopy(seq21_source)

   580	        assert self._activation_would_be_refused(mutated, seq20_source)

   636	    def test_no_review_rule_survives_on_requested_product_code_for_a_target_product(
   637	        self, seq21_source: dict[str, Any]
   638	    ) -> None:
   639	        """GUILT for A4: a REQUIRE_REVIEW rule that is UNKNOWN on
   640	        ``intent.requested_product_code`` makes its product BLOCKED_UNKNOWN
   641	        BEFORE ``purposes <= covered`` is ever tested
   642	        (``evaluator.evaluate_product``), so one left behind would make the
   643	        nine new SUPPORT rules structurally unable to produce a candidate."""
   644	        products = {
   645	            product["product_code"]: product["product_version_id"]
   646	            for product in seq21_source["products"]
   647	        }
   648	        targets = {products[code] for code in TARGET_PRODUCT_CODES}
   649	        offenders = [
   650	            rule["rule_id"]
   651	            for rule in seq21_source["rules"]
   652	            if rule["stage"] == "HUMAN_REVIEW"
   653	            and rule["on_unknown"] != "NO_EFFECT"
   654	            and "intent.requested_product_code" in rule["required_facts"]
   655	            and targets & set(rule["product_version_ids"])
   656	        ]
   657	        assert offenders == []

   703	    def test_every_support_rule_reads_its_own_qualifying_fact(
   704	        self, seq21_source: dict[str, Any]
   705	    ) -> None:

   707	        ``sponsor.type`` (or a route) alone."""

   722	    def test_sponsor_premises_admit_only_the_catalogues_sponsor_types(
   723	        self, seq20_source: dict[str, Any], seq21_source: dict[str, Any]
   724	    ) -> None:
   725	        """INNOCENCE on the base pack, then measured on the output: every
   726	        ``sponsor.type`` premise of a SUPPORT rule is a subset of its product's
   727	        catalogue ``sponsor_types`` (E33B -> NONE, E33C -> GOVERNMENT, never
   728	        the wider seq-20 hard filters' GOVERNMENT-or-NONE)."""
   729	        _assert_sponsor_premises_follow_the_catalogue(seq20_source)
   730	        products = {p["product_code"]: p for p in seq21_source["products"]}
   731	        rules = {r["rule_id"]: r for r in seq21_source["rules"]}
   732	        for row in SUPPORT_RULES:
   733	            for node in rules[row["rule_id"]]["when"]["args"]:
   734	                if node.get("fact") != "sponsor.type":
   735	                    continue
   736	                admitted = {node["value"]} if node["op"] == "eq" else set(node["values"])
   737	                assert admitted <= set(products[row["product_code"]]["sponsor_types"])

  1009	class TestCensusReplay:
  1010	    def test_no_walk_changes_state_or_candidates(
  1011	        self,
  1012	        seq20_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1013	        seq21_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1014	        walks: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
  1015	    ) -> None:
  1016	        """The honest measurement, and it is a NEGATIVE one: the corpus does
  1017	        not yet carry the ten new facts (W-VO-Q adds the questions), so no walk
  1018	        gains a product. What must NOT happen is a regression — a walk losing
  1019	        its answer, or an ``on_unknown: NEEDS_INPUT`` rule turning a decided
  1020	        dead end into a question."""
  1021	        before = _replay(seq20_compiled, walks)
  1022	        after = _replay(seq21_compiled, walks)
  1023	        drift = [
  1024	            f"{label}: {before[label]['state']} {before[label]['candidates']} -> "
  1025	            f"{after[label]['state']} {after[label]['candidates']}"
  1026	            for label in sorted(before)
  1027	            if (before[label]["state"], before[label]["candidates"])
  1028	            != (after[label]["state"], after[label]["candidates"])
  1029	        ]
  1030	        assert drift == []
  1031	
  1032	    def test_the_census_is_still_67_answers_15_no_paths_2_questions(
  1033	        self,
  1034	        seq21_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1035	        walks: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
  1036	    ) -> None:
  1037	        census = Counter(actual["state"] for actual in _replay(seq21_compiled, walks).values())
  1038	        assert census["SUPPORTED_CANDIDATES"] == 67
  1039	        assert census["NO_SUPPORTED_PATH"] == 15
  1040	        assert census["NEEDS_INPUT"] == 2
  1041	        assert census["HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED"] == 0
  1042	
  1043	    def test_no_walk_regresses_onto_requested_product_code(
  1044	        self,
  1045	        seq21_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1046	        walks: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
  1047	    ) -> None:
  1048	        """A4. ``intent.requested_product_code`` is never asked by the browser,
  1049	        so a walk blocked on it is a question the funnel cannot honour."""
  1050	        offenders = [
  1051	            label
  1052	            for label, actual in _replay(seq21_compiled, walks).items()
  1053	            if "intent.requested_product_code" in actual["missing_facts"]
  1054	        ]
  1055	        assert offenders == []
  1056	
  1057	    def test_the_three_generic_dead_ends_now_name_their_cause(
  1058	        self,
  1059	        seq20_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1060	        seq21_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1061	        walks: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
  1062	    ) -> None:
  1063	        before = _replay(seq20_compiled, walks)
  1064	        after = _replay(seq21_compiled, walks)
  1065	        for label, expected_code in NAMED_CAUSE_WALKS.items():
  1066	            assert before[label]["no_path_reason_codes"] == [_GENERIC_CAUSE], label
  1067	            codes = after[label]["no_path_reason_codes"]
  1068	            assert _GENERIC_CAUSE not in codes, label
  1069	            assert expected_code in codes, label
  1070	            assert after[label]["state"] == "NO_SUPPORTED_PATH", label
  1071	
  1072	    def test_no_other_walk_loses_or_gains_a_no_path_reason(
  1073	        self,
  1074	        seq20_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1075	        seq21_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,

  1079	        written for and nothing else."""

  1086	        )

  1089	    def test_every_no_path_code_seq21_emits_on_the_corpus_has_visitor_copy(
  1090	        self,
  1091	        seq21_compiled: compiler.CompiledRulePack,
  1092	        walks: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
  1093	    ) -> None:
  1094	        """Cross-app guard: a no-path code without an entry in the mouth's
  1095	        ``SUPPORT_REASON_COPY`` renders as ``Verified reason: <CODE>``. seq-21
  1096	        makes E33A purpose-feasible on a paid-work walk, which surfaces the
  1097	        seq-20 code ``E33A_SPONSOR_NOT_GOVERNMENT`` there for the first time;
  1098	        this measures every code the candidate emits on the 84 walks against
  1099	        the copy keys parsed out of ``engine-adapter.ts``."""
  1100	        adapter = (
  1101	            Path(__file__).resolve().parents[5]
  1102	            / "mouth/src/app/(visa-oracle)/visa-oracle/_lib/engine-adapter.ts"
  1103	        ).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  1104	        start = adapter.index("export const SUPPORT_REASON_COPY")
  1105	        body = adapter[start : adapter.index("\n};", start)]
  1106	        copy_keys = set(re.findall(r"^  ([A-Z0-9_]+): text\(", body, re.MULTILINE))
  1107	        assert len(copy_keys) > 20, "the copy-map parse found almost nothing"
  1108	        emitted = {
  1109	            code
  1110	            for outcome in _replay(seq21_compiled, walks).values()
  1111	            for code in outcome["no_path_reason_codes"]
  1112	        }
  1113	        assert emitted, "no walk emitted a no-path code — the replay proves nothing"
  1114	        assert sorted(emitted - copy_keys) == []

   230	def _decide(
   231	    pack: compiler.CompiledRulePack, overrides: dict[str, Any], label: str
   232	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   233	    """The applicant-facing outcome: the same verify-free compile → evaluate →
   234	    ``apply_public_policy_adapters`` path ``gold_coverage_eval._evaluate``
   235	    walks, with the pack handed in rather than selected from disk (the
   236	    candidate is unsigned, so the selector cannot see it)."""
   237	    persona = Persona(
   238	        id=0, label=label, overrides=overrides, expected_state=DecisionState.NEEDS_INPUT
   239	    )
   240	    request = build_persona_request(persona)
   241	    facts = request.applicant_facts()
   242	    decision = evaluator.evaluate(
   243	        facts,
   244	        pack,
   245	        effective_at=AS_OF,
   246	        observed_at=AS_OF,
   247	        identity_provider=_offline_identity_provider,
   248	    )
   249	    decision = evaluate_path.apply_public_policy_adapters(
   250	        decision,
   251	        facts,
   252	        pack,
   253	        disclosed_review_flags=request.effective_review_flags(),
   254	    )
   255	    return _decision_actual(decision)
